codex
The original layout is z-major, so I’m using a balanced z-slab decomposition: each rank owns contiguous whole xy planes, with two ghost planes. That keeps x-neighbor accesses contiguous, makes halo messages contiguous (no packing/datatype overhead), and allows communication to overlap computation on the slab interior. Only rank 0 will retain the program’s normal console/output behavior.

 succeeded in 0ms:
#pragma once

#include <algorithm>
#include <cinttypes>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdio>
#include <vector>

#include "hash.hpp"

// Kahan summation for numerically stable sum
template <typename T>
T kahan_sum(const std::vector<T>& data) {
    T sum = 0.0;
    T c = 0.0;
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        T y = val - c;
        T t = sum + y;
        c = (t - sum) - y;
        sum = t;
    }
    return sum;
}

// Print results for floating-point arrays
template <typename T>
void print_results(const std::vector<T>& data, const char* name = "Result") {
    if (data.empty()) {
        printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
        printf("Name: %s\n", name);
        printf("Elements: 0\n");
        printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
        return;
    }

    const size_t N = data.size();
    
    // Compute statistics
    T sum = kahan_sum(data);
    T min_val = data[0];
    T max_val = data[0];
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        min_val = std::min(min_val, val);
        max_val = std::max(max_val, val);
    }
    
    // Compute hash
    hash h;
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        h.add(val);
    }
    
    // Sample indices: 0, N/4, N/2, 3N/4, N-1
    size_t indices[5] = {0, N/4, N/2, 3*N/4, N-1};
    
    printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
    printf("Name: %s\n", name);
    printf("Elements: %zu\n", N);
    printf("Sum: %.17e\n", static_cast<double>(sum));
    printf("Min: %.17e\n", static_cast<double>(min_val));
    printf("Max: %.17e\n", static_cast<double>(max_val));
    for (int i = 0; i < 5; ++i) {
        printf("Sample[%zu]: %.17e\n", indices[i], static_cast<double>(data[indices[i]]));
    }
    printf("Hash: %016" PRIx64 "\n", h.get());
    printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
}

// Print results for integer arrays (uses exact hash comparison)
template <typename T>
void print_results_int(const std::vector<T>& data, const char* name = "Result") {
    if (data.empty()) {
        printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
        printf("Name: %s\n", name);
        printf("Elements: 0\n");
        printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
        return;
    }

    const size_t N = data.size();
    
    // Compute hash
    hash h;
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        h.add(val);
    }
    
    // Compute min/max
    T min_val = data[0];
    T max_val = data[0];
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        min_val = std::min(min_val, val);
        max_val = std::max(max_val, val);
    }
    
    // Sample indices: 0, N/4, N/2, 3N/4, N-1
    size_t indices[5] = {0, N/4, N/2, 3*N/4, N-1};
    
    printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
    printf("Name: %s\n", name);
    printf("Elements: %zu\n", N);
    printf("Min: %lld\n", static_cast<long long>(min_val));
    printf("Max: %lld\n", static_cast<long long>(max_val));
    for (int i = 0; i < 5; ++i) {
        printf("Sample[%zu]: %lld\n", indices[i], static_cast<long long>(data[indices[i]]));
    }
    printf("Hash: %016" PRIx64 "\n", h.get());
    printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
}

-void initializeGrid(std::vector<Real>& grid, const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz) {

+struct Slab {
+    size_t firstZ;
+    size_t nz;
+};
+
+static Slab decompose(const size_t nz, const int rank, const int ranks) {
+    const size_t base = nz / static_cast<size_t>(ranks);
+    const size_t extra = nz % static_cast<size_t>(ranks);
+    const size_t r = static_cast<size_t>(rank);
+    return {r * base + std::min(r, extra), base + (r < extra ? 1U : 0U)};
+}
+
+static void initializeGrid(std::vector<Real>& grid, const size_t nx,
+                           const size_t ny, const Slab slab) {
+    const size_t plane = nx * ny;
+    for (size_t lz = 1; lz <= slab.nz; ++lz) {
+        const size_t globalBase = (slab.firstZ + lz - 1) * plane;
+        Real* const dst = grid.data() + lz * plane;
+        for (size_t i = 0; i < plane; ++i)
+            dst[i] = static_cast<Real>((globalBase + i) % 19);
     }
 }

+// Update one owned plane.  Physical boundary cells retain their input value.
+static inline void updatePlane(const Real* input, Real* output,
+                               const size_t lz, const size_t globalZ,
+                               const size_t nx, const size_t ny,
+                               const size_t globalNz) {
+    const size_t plane = nx * ny;
+    const size_t base = lz * plane;
+    if (globalZ == 0 || globalZ + 1 == globalNz) {
+        std::memcpy(output + base, input + base, plane * sizeof(Real));
+        return;
+    }
+
+    // Copy the four xy boundary edges (corners may harmlessly be copied twice).
+    std::memcpy(output + base, input + base, nx * sizeof(Real));
+    std::memcpy(output + base + (ny - 1) * nx,
+                input + base + (ny - 1) * nx, nx * sizeof(Real));
+    for (size_t y = 1; y + 1 < ny; ++y) {
+        output[base + y * nx] = input[base + y * nx];
+        output[base + y * nx + nx - 1] = input[base + y * nx + nx - 1];
     }
-    
-    // Copy boundary values
-    for (size_t z = 0; z < nz; ++z) {
-        for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {
-            for (size_t x = 0; x < nx; ++x) {
-                if (x == 0 || x == nx-1 || y == 0 || y == ny-1 || z == 0 || z == nz-1) {
-                    const size_t idx = idx3(x, y, z, nx, ny);
-                    output[idx] = input[idx];
-                }
-            }
+
+    constexpr Real oneSeventh = Real{1.0 / 7.0};
+    for (size_t y = 1; y + 1 < ny; ++y) {
+        const size_t row = base + y * nx;
+        for (size_t x = 1; x + 1 < nx; ++x) {
+            const size_t i = row + x;
+            output[i] = (input[i] + input[i - 1] + input[i + 1] +
+                         input[i - nx] + input[i + nx] +
+                         input[i - plane] + input[i + plane]) * oneSeventh;
         }
     }
 }

+static void stencilIteration(const std::vector<Real>& input,
+                             std::vector<Real>& output, const size_t nx,
+                             const size_t ny, const size_t globalNz,
+                             const Slab slab, const int rank, const int ranks,
+                             MPI_Comm comm) {
+    const size_t plane = nx * ny;
+    MPI_Request requests[4];
+    int nreq = 0;
+
+    // Receive directly into ghost planes and send directly from owned planes.
+    if (rank > 0) {
+        MPI_Irecv(const_cast<Real*>(input.data()), static_cast<int>(plane),
+                  MPI_DOUBLE, rank - 1, 101, comm, &requests[nreq++]);
+        MPI_Isend(input.data() + plane, static_cast<int>(plane), MPI_DOUBLE,
+                  rank - 1, 102, comm, &requests[nreq++]);
+    }
+    if (rank + 1 < ranks) {
+        MPI_Irecv(const_cast<Real*>(input.data()) + (slab.nz + 1) * plane,
+                  static_cast<int>(plane), MPI_DOUBLE, rank + 1, 102, comm,
+                  &requests[nreq++]);
+        MPI_Isend(input.data() + slab.nz * plane, static_cast<int>(plane),
+                  MPI_DOUBLE, rank + 1, 101, comm, &requests[nreq++]);
+    }
+
+    // These planes depend only on locally owned data, overlapping halo traffic.
+    for (size_t lz = 2; lz < slab.nz; ++lz)
+        updatePlane(input.data(), output.data(), lz, slab.firstZ + lz - 1,
+                    nx, ny, globalNz);
+
+    if (nreq != 0)
+        MPI_Waitall(nreq, requests, MPI_STATUSES_IGNORE);
+
+    updatePlane(input.data(), output.data(), 1, slab.firstZ,
+                nx, ny, globalNz);
+    if (slab.nz > 1)
+        updatePlane(input.data(), output.data(), slab.nz,
+                    slab.firstZ + slab.nz - 1, nx, ny, globalNz);
+}

+// Update one owned plane.  Physical boundary cells retain their input value.
+static inline void updatePlane(const Real* input, Real* output,
+                               const size_t lz, const size_t globalZ,
+                               const size_t nx, const size_t ny,
+                               const size_t globalNz) {
+    const size_t plane = nx * ny;
+    const size_t base = lz * plane;
+    if (globalZ == 0 || globalZ + 1 == globalNz) {
+        std::memcpy(output + base, input + base, plane * sizeof(Real));
+        return;
+    }
+
+    // Copy the four xy boundary edges (corners may harmlessly be copied twice).
+    std::memcpy(output + base, input + base, nx * sizeof(Real));
+    std::memcpy(output + base + (ny - 1) * nx,
+                input + base + (ny - 1) * nx, nx * sizeof(Real));
+    for (size_t y = 1; y + 1 < ny; ++y) {
+        output[base + y * nx] = input[base + y * nx];
+        output[base + y * nx + nx - 1] = input[base + y * nx + nx - 1];
     }
-    
-    // Copy boundary values
-    for (size_t z = 0; z < nz; ++z) {
-        for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {
-            for (size_t x = 0; x < nx; ++x) {
-                if (x == 0 || x == nx-1 || y == 0 || y == ny-1 || z == 0 || z == nz-1) {
-                    const size_t idx = idx3(x, y, z, nx, ny);
-                    output[idx] = input[idx];
-                }
-            }
+
+    constexpr Real oneSeventh = Real{1.0 / 7.0};
+    for (size_t y = 1; y + 1 < ny; ++y) {
+        const size_t row = base + y * nx;
+        for (size_t x = 1; x + 1 < nx; ++x) {
+            const size_t i = row + x;
+            output[i] = (input[i] + input[i - 1] + input[i + 1] +
+                         input[i - nx] + input[i + nx] +
+                         input[i - plane] + input[i + plane]) * oneSeventh;
         }
     }
 }

+static void stencilIteration(const std::vector<Real>& input,
+                             std::vector<Real>& output, const size_t nx,
+                             const size_t ny, const size_t globalNz,
+                             const Slab slab, const int rank, const int ranks,
+                             MPI_Comm comm) {
+    const size_t plane = nx * ny;
+    MPI_Request requests[4];
+    int nreq = 0;
+
+    // Receive directly into ghost planes and send directly from owned planes.
+    if (rank > 0) {
+        MPI_Irecv(const_cast<Real*>(input.data()), static_cast<int>(plane),
+                  MPI_DOUBLE, rank - 1, 101, comm, &requests[nreq++]);
+        MPI_Isend(input.data() + plane, static_cast<int>(plane), MPI_DOUBLE,
+                  rank - 1, 102, comm, &requests[nreq++]);
+    }
+    if (rank + 1 < ranks) {
+        MPI_Irecv(const_cast<Real*>(input.data()) + (slab.nz + 1) * plane,
+                  static_cast<int>(plane), MPI_DOUBLE, rank + 1, 102, comm,
+                  &requests[nreq++]);
+        MPI_Isend(input.data() + slab.nz * plane, static_cast<int>(plane),
+                  MPI_DOUBLE, rank + 1, 101, comm, &requests[nreq++]);
+    }
+
+    // These planes depend only on locally owned data, overlapping halo traffic.
+    for (size_t lz = 2; lz < slab.nz; ++lz)
+        updatePlane(input.data(), output.data(), lz, slab.firstZ + lz - 1,
+                    nx, ny, globalNz);
+
+    if (nreq != 0)
+        MPI_Waitall(nreq, requests, MPI_STATUSES_IGNORE);
+
+    updatePlane(input.data(), output.data(), 1, slab.firstZ,
+                nx, ny, globalNz);
+    if (slab.nz > 1)
+        updatePlane(input.data(), output.data(), slab.nz,
+                    slab.firstZ + slab.nz - 1, nx, ny, globalNz);
+}

The MPI version is now in place. I’ve kept the numerical operation order per cell unchanged, and gathering occurs only for `-r`, so normal benchmark runs stay fully distributed. I’m moving through build warnings and cross-rank hash comparisons now, including uneven slab sizes and more ranks than z planes.

+static Slab decompose(const size_t nz, const int rank, const int ranks) {
+    const size_t base = nz / static_cast<size_t>(ranks);
+    const size_t extra = nz % static_cast<size_t>(ranks);
+    const size_t r = static_cast<size_t>(rank);
+    return {r * base + std::min(r, extra), base + (r < extra ? 1U : 0U)};
+}

+static void initializeGrid(std::vector<Real>& grid, const size_t nx,
+                           const size_t ny, const Slab slab) {

+    for (size_t lz = 1; lz <= slab.nz; ++lz) {

+// Update one owned plane.  Physical boundary cells retain their input value.
+static inline void updatePlane(const Real* input, Real* output,
+                               const size_t lz, const size_t globalZ,
+                               const size_t nx, const size_t ny,

+static void stencilIteration(const std::vector<Real>& input,
+                             std::vector<Real>& output, const size_t nx,
+                             const size_t ny, const size_t globalNz,
+                             const Slab slab, const int rank, const int ranks,
+                             MPI_Comm comm) {
+    const size_t plane = nx * ny;
+    MPI_Request requests[4];
+    int nreq = 0;
+
+    // Receive directly into ghost planes and send directly from owned planes.
+    if (rank > 0) {
+        MPI_Irecv(const_cast<Real*>(input.data()), static_cast<int>(plane),
+                  MPI_DOUBLE, rank - 1, 101, comm, &requests[nreq++]);
+        MPI_Isend(input.data() + plane, static_cast<int>(plane), MPI_DOUBLE,
+                  rank - 1, 102, comm, &requests[nreq++]);
+    }
+    if (rank + 1 < ranks) {
+        MPI_Irecv(const_cast<Real*>(input.data()) + (slab.nz + 1) * plane,
+                  static_cast<int>(plane), MPI_DOUBLE, rank + 1, 102, comm,
+                  &requests[nreq++]);
+        MPI_Isend(input.data() + slab.nz * plane, static_cast<int>(plane),
+                  MPI_DOUBLE, rank + 1, 101, comm, &requests[nreq++]);
+    }
+
+    // These planes depend only on locally owned data, overlapping halo traffic.
+    for (size_t lz = 2; lz < slab.nz; ++lz)
+        updatePlane(input.data(), output.data(), lz, slab.firstZ + lz - 1,
+                    nx, ny, globalNz);
+
+    if (nreq != 0)
+        MPI_Waitall(nreq, requests, MPI_STATUSES_IGNORE);
+
+    updatePlane(input.data(), output.data(), 1, slab.firstZ,
+                nx, ny, globalNz);
+    if (slab.nz > 1)
+        updatePlane(input.data(), output.data(), slab.nz,
+                    slab.firstZ + slab.nz - 1, nx, ny, globalNz);
+}

+static Slab decompose(const size_t nz, const int rank, const int ranks) {
+    const size_t base = nz / static_cast<size_t>(ranks);
+    const size_t extra = nz % static_cast<size_t>(ranks);
+    const size_t r = static_cast<size_t>(rank);
+    return {r * base + std::min(r, extra), base + (r < extra ? 1U : 0U)};
+}
+
+static void initializeGrid(std::vector<Real>& grid, const size_t nx,
+                           const size_t ny, const Slab slab) {
+    const size_t plane = nx * ny;
+    for (size_t lz = 1; lz <= slab.nz; ++lz) {
+        const size_t globalBase = (slab.firstZ + lz - 1) * plane;

+        for (size_t i = 0; i < plane; ++i)
+            dst[i] = static_cast<Real>((globalBase + i) % 19);
     }

+// Update one owned plane.  Physical boundary cells retain their input value.
+static inline void updatePlane(const Real* input, Real* output,
+                               const size_t lz, const size_t globalZ,
+                               const size_t nx, const size_t ny,
+                               const size_t globalNz) {
+    const size_t plane = nx * ny;
+    const size_t base = lz * plane;
+    if (globalZ == 0 || globalZ + 1 == globalNz) {
+        std::memcpy(output + base, input + base, plane * sizeof(Real));
+        return;
+    }
+
+    // Copy the four xy boundary edges (corners may harmlessly be copied twice).
+    std::memcpy(output + base, input + base, nx * sizeof(Real));
+    std::memcpy(output + base + (ny - 1) * nx,
+                input + base + (ny - 1) * nx, nx * sizeof(Real));
+    for (size_t y = 1; y + 1 < ny; ++y) {
+        output[base + y * nx] = input[base + y * nx];
+        output[base + y * nx + nx - 1] = input[base + y * nx + nx - 1];
     }
-    
-    // Copy boundary values
-    for (size_t z = 0; z < nz; ++z) {
-        for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {
-            for (size_t x = 0; x < nx; ++x) {
-                if (x == 0 || x == nx-1 || y == 0 || y == ny-1 || z == 0 || z == nz-1) {
-                    const size_t idx = idx3(x, y, z, nx, ny);
-                    output[idx] = input[idx];
-                }
-            }
+
+    constexpr Real oneSeventh = Real{1.0 / 7.0};
+    for (size_t y = 1; y + 1 < ny; ++y) {
+        const size_t row = base + y * nx;
+        for (size_t x = 1; x + 1 < nx; ++x) {
+            const size_t i = row + x;
+            output[i] = (input[i] + input[i - 1] + input[i + 1] +
+                         input[i - nx] + input[i + nx] +
+                         input[i - plane] + input[i + plane]) * oneSeventh;
         }
     }
 }

-        if (std::isnan(val) || std::isinf(val)) {

+static void stencilIteration(const std::vector<Real>& input,
+                             std::vector<Real>& output, const size_t nx,
+                             const size_t ny, const size_t globalNz,
+                             const Slab slab, const int rank, const int ranks,
+                             MPI_Comm comm) {
+    const size_t plane = nx * ny;
+    MPI_Request requests[4];
+    int nreq = 0;
+
+    // Receive directly into ghost planes and send directly from owned planes.
+    if (rank > 0) {
+        MPI_Irecv(const_cast<Real*>(input.data()), static_cast<int>(plane),
+                  MPI_DOUBLE, rank - 1, 101, comm, &requests[nreq++]);
+        MPI_Isend(input.data() + plane, static_cast<int>(plane), MPI_DOUBLE,
+                  rank - 1, 102, comm, &requests[nreq++]);
+    }
+    if (rank + 1 < ranks) {
+        MPI_Irecv(const_cast<Real*>(input.data()) + (slab.nz + 1) * plane,
+                  static_cast<int>(plane), MPI_DOUBLE, rank + 1, 102, comm,
+                  &requests[nreq++]);
+        MPI_Isend(input.data() + slab.nz * plane, static_cast<int>(plane),
+                  MPI_DOUBLE, rank + 1, 101, comm, &requests[nreq++]);
+    }
+
+    // These planes depend only on locally owned data, overlapping halo traffic.
+    for (size_t lz = 2; lz < slab.nz; ++lz)
+        updatePlane(input.data(), output.data(), lz, slab.firstZ + lz - 1,
+                    nx, ny, globalNz);
+
+    if (nreq != 0)
+        MPI_Waitall(nreq, requests, MPI_STATUSES_IGNORE);
+
+    updatePlane(input.data(), output.data(), 1, slab.firstZ,
+                nx, ny, globalNz);
+    if (slab.nz > 1)
+        updatePlane(input.data(), output.data(), slab.nz,
+                    slab.firstZ + slab.nz - 1, nx, ny, globalNz);
+}

+static bool validateResult(const std::vector<Real>& grid, const size_t plane,
+                           const size_t localNz, const int rank, MPI_Comm comm) {
+    int localBad = 0;
+    Real localMin = std::numeric_limits<Real>::infinity();
+    Real localMax = -std::numeric_limits<Real>::infinity();
+    for (size_t i = plane; i < (localNz + 1) * plane; ++i) {
+        const Real value = grid[i];
+        localBad |= !std::isfinite(value);
+        localMin = std::min(localMin, value);
+        localMax = std::max(localMax, value);
     }

-    // 2. Values should be reasonable (bounded)

-    Real maxVal = grid[0];
-    for (const auto& val : grid) {

-        maxVal = std::max(maxVal, val);
+
+    int bad = 0;
+    Real minVal = 0.0, maxVal = 0.0;
+    MPI_Reduce(&localBad, &bad, 1, MPI_INT, MPI_LOR, 0, comm);
+    MPI_Reduce(&localMin, &minVal, 1, MPI_DOUBLE, MPI_MIN, 0, comm);
+    MPI_Reduce(&localMax, &maxVal, 1, MPI_DOUBLE, MPI_MAX, 0, comm);
+    if (rank != 0) return true;
+
+    if (bad) {
+        std::printf("Validation failed: found NaN or Inf value\n");
+        return false;
     }
-    
-    printf("Value range: [%.6f, %.6f]\n", minVal, maxVal);
-    
-    // After averaging, values should be somewhat bounded
+    std::printf("Value range: [%.6f, %.6f]\n", minVal, maxVal);
     if (maxVal > 1e6 || minVal < -1e6) {

+        std::printf("Validation failed: values out of expected range\n");

-    // (they are copied, so they should be close to initial values)
-    
     return true;
 }

+static Slab decompose(const size_t nz, const int rank, const int ranks) {
+    const size_t base = nz / static_cast<size_t>(ranks);
+    const size_t extra = nz % static_cast<size_t>(ranks);
+    const size_t r = static_cast<size_t>(rank);
+    return {r * base + std::min(r, extra), base + (r < extra ? 1U : 0U)};

+static void initializeGrid(std::vector<Real>& grid, const size_t nx,
+                           const size_t ny, const Slab slab) {
+    const size_t plane = nx * ny;
+    for (size_t lz = 1; lz <= slab.nz; ++lz) {
+        const size_t globalBase = (slab.firstZ + lz - 1) * plane;
+        Real* const dst = grid.data() + lz * plane;
+        for (size_t i = 0; i < plane; ++i)
+            dst[i] = static_cast<Real>((globalBase + i) % 19);
     }

+static inline void updatePlane(const Real* input, Real* output,
+                               const size_t lz, const size_t globalZ,
+                               const size_t nx, const size_t ny,
+                               const size_t globalNz) {
+    const size_t plane = nx * ny;
+    const size_t base = lz * plane;
+    if (globalZ == 0 || globalZ + 1 == globalNz) {
+        std::memcpy(output + base, input + base, plane * sizeof(Real));

+                input + base + (ny - 1) * nx, nx * sizeof(Real));

+struct Slab {

+static Slab decompose(const size_t nz, const int rank, const int ranks) {
+    const size_t base = nz / static_cast<size_t>(ranks);
+    const size_t extra = nz % static_cast<size_t>(ranks);
+    const size_t r = static_cast<size_t>(rank);
+    return {r * base + std::min(r, extra), base + (r < extra ? 1U : 0U)};
+}
+
+static void initializeGrid(std::vector<Real>& grid, const size_t nx,
+                           const size_t ny, const Slab slab) {
+    const size_t plane = nx * ny;
+    for (size_t lz = 1; lz <= slab.nz; ++lz) {
+        const size_t globalBase = (slab.firstZ + lz - 1) * plane;
+        Real* const dst = grid.data() + lz * plane;
+        for (size_t i = 0; i < plane; ++i)
+            dst[i] = static_cast<Real>((globalBase + i) % 19);
+    }
+}
+
+// Update one owned plane.  Physical boundary cells retain their input value.
+static inline void updatePlane(const Real* input, Real* output,
+                               const size_t lz, const size_t globalZ,
+                               const size_t nx, const size_t ny,
+                               const size_t globalNz) {
+    const size_t plane = nx * ny;
+    const size_t base = lz * plane;
+    if (globalZ == 0 || globalZ + 1 == globalNz) {
+        std::memcpy(output + base, input + base, plane * sizeof(Real));
+        return;
+    }
+
+    // Copy the four xy boundary edges (corners may harmlessly be copied twice).
+    std::memcpy(output + base, input + base, nx * sizeof(Real));
+    std::memcpy(output + base + (ny - 1) * nx,
+                input + base + (ny - 1) * nx, nx * sizeof(Real));
+    for (size_t y = 1; y + 1 < ny; ++y) {
+        output[base + y * nx] = input[base + y * nx];
+        output[base + y * nx + nx - 1] = input[base + y * nx + nx - 1];
+    }
+
+    constexpr Real oneSeventh = Real{1.0 / 7.0};
+    for (size_t y = 1; y + 1 < ny; ++y) {
+        const size_t row = base + y * nx;
+        for (size_t x = 1; x + 1 < nx; ++x) {
+            const size_t i = row + x;
+            output[i] = (input[i] + input[i - 1] + input[i + 1] +
+                         input[i - nx] + input[i + nx] +
+                         input[i - plane] + input[i + plane]) * oneSeventh;
         }
     }

+    MPI_Request requests[4];
+    int nreq = 0;
+

+                  MPI_DOUBLE, rank - 1, 101, comm, &requests[nreq++]);

+                  &requests[nreq++]);

+                  MPI_DOUBLE, rank + 1, 101, comm, &requests[nreq++]);
     }
+
+    // These planes depend only on locally owned data, overlapping halo traffic.

+

-void initializeGrid(std::vector<Real>& grid, const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz) {

+struct Slab {
+    size_t firstZ;
+    size_t nz;
+};
+
+static Slab decompose(const size_t nz, const int rank, const int ranks) {
+    const size_t base = nz / static_cast<size_t>(ranks);
+    const size_t extra = nz % static_cast<size_t>(ranks);
+    const size_t r = static_cast<size_t>(rank);
+    return {r * base + std::min(r, extra), base + (r < extra ? 1U : 0U)};
+}
+
+static void initializeGrid(std::vector<Real>& grid, const size_t nx,
+                           const size_t ny, const Slab slab) {
+    const size_t plane = nx * ny;
+    for (size_t lz = 1; lz <= slab.nz; ++lz) {
+        const size_t globalBase = (slab.firstZ + lz - 1) * plane;
+        Real* const dst = grid.data() + lz * plane;
+        for (size_t i = 0; i < plane; ++i)
+            dst[i] = static_cast<Real>((globalBase + i) % 19);
+    }
+}
+
+// Update one owned plane.  Physical boundary cells retain their input value.
+static inline void updatePlane(const Real* input, Real* output,
+                               const size_t lz, const size_t globalZ,
+                               const size_t nx, const size_t ny,
+                               const size_t globalNz) {
+    const size_t plane = nx * ny;
+    const size_t base = lz * plane;
+    if (globalZ == 0 || globalZ + 1 == globalNz) {
+        std::memcpy(output + base, input + base, plane * sizeof(Real));
+        return;
+    }
+
+    // Copy the four xy boundary edges (corners may harmlessly be copied twice).
+    std::memcpy(output + base, input + base, nx * sizeof(Real));
+    std::memcpy(output + base + (ny - 1) * nx,
+                input + base + (ny - 1) * nx, nx * sizeof(Real));
+    for (size_t y = 1; y + 1 < ny; ++y) {
+        output[base + y * nx] = input[base + y * nx];
+        output[base + y * nx + nx - 1] = input[base + y * nx + nx - 1];
+    }
+
+    constexpr Real oneSeventh = Real{1.0 / 7.0};
+    for (size_t y = 1; y + 1 < ny; ++y) {
+        const size_t row = base + y * nx;
+        for (size_t x = 1; x + 1 < nx; ++x) {
+            const size_t i = row + x;
+            output[i] = (input[i] + input[i - 1] + input[i + 1] +
+                         input[i - nx] + input[i + nx] +
+                         input[i - plane] + input[i + plane]) * oneSeventh;
         }
     }
 }

+        MPI_Isend(input.data() + slab.nz * plane, static_cast<int>(plane),
+                  MPI_DOUBLE, rank + 1, 101, comm, &requests[nreq++]);
     }
+
+    // These planes depend only on locally owned data, overlapping halo traffic.
+    for (size_t lz = 2; lz < slab.nz; ++lz)
+        updatePlane(input.data(), output.data(), lz, slab.firstZ + lz - 1,
+                    nx, ny, globalNz);
+
+    if (nreq != 0)
+        MPI_Waitall(nreq, requests, MPI_STATUSES_IGNORE);
+
+    updatePlane(input.data(), output.data(), 1, slab.firstZ,
+                nx, ny, globalNz);
+    if (slab.nz > 1)
+        updatePlane(input.data(), output.data(), slab.nz,
+                    slab.firstZ + slab.nz - 1, nx, ny, globalNz);
 }

-void initializeGrid(std::vector<Real>& grid, const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz) {
-    for (size_t z = 0; z < nz; ++z) {
-        for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {

-                grid[idx] = (idx % 19) * 1.0;
-            }
+struct Slab {
+    size_t firstZ;
+    size_t nz;
+};
+
+static Slab decompose(const size_t nz, const int rank, const int ranks) {
+    const size_t base = nz / static_cast<size_t>(ranks);
+    const size_t extra = nz % static_cast<size_t>(ranks);
+    const size_t r = static_cast<size_t>(rank);
+    return {r * base + std::min(r, extra), base + (r < extra ? 1U : 0U)};
+}
+
+static void initializeGrid(std::vector<Real>& grid, const size_t nx,
+                           const size_t ny, const Slab slab) {
+    const size_t plane = nx * ny;
+    for (size_t lz = 1; lz <= slab.nz; ++lz) {
+        const size_t globalBase = (slab.firstZ + lz - 1) * plane;
+        Real* const dst = grid.data() + lz * plane;
+        for (size_t i = 0; i < plane; ++i)
+            dst[i] = static_cast<Real>((globalBase + i) % 19);
+    }
+}
+
+// Update one owned plane.  Physical boundary cells retain their input value.
+static inline void updatePlane(const Real* input, Real* output,
+                               const size_t lz, const size_t globalZ,
+                               const size_t nx, const size_t ny,
+                               const size_t globalNz) {
+    const size_t plane = nx * ny;
+    const size_t base = lz * plane;
+    if (globalZ == 0 || globalZ + 1 == globalNz) {
+        std::memcpy(output + base, input + base, plane * sizeof(Real));
+        return;
+    }
+
+    // Copy the four xy boundary edges (corners may harmlessly be copied twice).
+    std::memcpy(output + base, input + base, nx * sizeof(Real));
+    std::memcpy(output + base + (ny - 1) * nx,
+                input + base + (ny - 1) * nx, nx * sizeof(Real));
+    for (size_t y = 1; y + 1 < ny; ++y) {
+        output[base + y * nx] = input[base + y * nx];
+        output[base + y * nx + nx - 1] = input[base + y * nx + nx - 1];
+    }
+
+    constexpr Real oneSeventh = Real{1.0 / 7.0};
+    for (size_t y = 1; y + 1 < ny; ++y) {
+        const size_t row = base + y * nx;
+        for (size_t x = 1; x + 1 < nx; ++x) {
+            const size_t i = row + x;
+            output[i] = (input[i] + input[i - 1] + input[i + 1] +
+                         input[i - nx] + input[i + nx] +
+                         input[i - plane] + input[i + plane]) * oneSeventh;
         }
     }
 }

+static void stencilIteration(std::vector<Real>& input,
+                             std::vector<Real>& output, const size_t nx,
+                             const size_t ny, const size_t globalNz,
+                             const Slab slab, const int rank, const int ranks,
+                             MPI_Comm comm) {
+    const size_t plane = nx * ny;
+    MPI_Request requests[4];
+    int nreq = 0;
+
+    // Receive directly into ghost planes and send directly from owned planes.
+    if (rank > 0) {
+        MPI_Irecv(input.data(), static_cast<int>(plane),
+                  MPI_DOUBLE, rank - 1, 101, comm, &requests[nreq++]);
+        MPI_Isend(input.data() + plane, static_cast<int>(plane), MPI_DOUBLE,
+                  rank - 1, 102, comm, &requests[nreq++]);
     }
-    
-    // Copy boundary values
-    for (size_t z = 0; z < nz; ++z) {

-                    const size_t idx = idx3(x, y, z, nx, ny);
-                    output[idx] = input[idx];
-                }
-            }
-        }
+    if (rank + 1 < ranks) {
+        MPI_Irecv(input.data() + (slab.nz + 1) * plane,
+                  static_cast<int>(plane), MPI_DOUBLE, rank + 1, 102, comm,
+                  &requests[nreq++]);
+        MPI_Isend(input.data() + slab.nz * plane, static_cast<int>(plane),
+                  MPI_DOUBLE, rank + 1, 101, comm, &requests[nreq++]);
     }
+
+    // These planes depend only on locally owned data, overlapping halo traffic.
+    for (size_t lz = 2; lz < slab.nz; ++lz)
+        updatePlane(input.data(), output.data(), lz, slab.firstZ + lz - 1,
+                    nx, ny, globalNz);
+
+    if (nreq != 0)
+        MPI_Waitall(nreq, requests, MPI_STATUSES_IGNORE);
+
+    updatePlane(input.data(), output.data(), 1, slab.firstZ,
+                nx, ny, globalNz);
+    if (slab.nz > 1)
+        updatePlane(input.data(), output.data(), slab.nz,
+                    slab.firstZ + slab.nz - 1, nx, ny, globalNz);
 }

    if (rank == 0) {
        std::printf("Computation time: %ld ms\n", static_cast<long>(elapsed * 1000.0));
        const double updates = static_cast<double>(nx - 2) *
                               static_cast<double>(ny - 2) *
                               static_cast<double>(nz - 2) * iterations;
        std::printf("Performance: %.3f MCellUpdates/s\n",
                    elapsed > 0.0 ? updates / elapsed / 1e6 : 0.0);
    }

    if (printResults) {
        std::vector<int> counts, displacements;
        std::vector<Real> gathered;
        const size_t totalElements = plane * nz;
        if (slab.nz * plane > static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max()) ||
            totalElements > static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max())) {
            if (rank == 0) std::fprintf(stderr, "Result gathering exceeds MPI count limits\n");
            if (rank == 0) std::fprintf(stderr, "Result gathering exceeds MPI count limits\n");
            MPI_Abort(comm, 1);
        }
        if (rank == 0) {
            counts.resize(activeRanks);
            displacements.resize(activeRanks);
            gathered.resize(totalElements);
            for (int r = 0; r < activeRanks; ++r) {
                const Slab s = decompose(nz, r, activeRanks);
                counts[r] = static_cast<int>(s.nz * plane);
                displacements[r] = static_cast<int>(s.firstZ * plane);
            }
        }
        MPI_Gatherv(finalGrid.data() + plane, static_cast<int>(slab.nz * plane),
                    MPI_DOUBLE, rank == 0 ? gathered.data() : nullptr,
                    rank == 0 ? counts.data() : nullptr,
                    rank == 0 ? displacements.data() : nullptr,
                    MPI_DOUBLE, 0, comm);
        if (rank == 0) print_results(gathered, "Grid");
    }

    int returnCode = 0;
    if (validate) {
        if (rank == 0) std::printf("Validating result...\n");
        const bool valid = validateResult(finalGrid, plane, slab.nz, rank, comm);
        if (rank == 0) {
            std::printf("Validation: %s\n", valid ? "PASSED" : "FAILED");
            returnCode = valid ? 0 : 1;
        }
        MPI_Bcast(&returnCode, 1, MPI_INT, 0, comm);
    }

    MPI_Comm_free(&comm);
    MPI_Finalize();
    return returnCode;
}

-void initializeGrid(std::vector<Real>& grid, const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz) {

+struct Slab {
+    size_t firstZ;
+    size_t nz;
+};
+
+static Slab decompose(const size_t nz, const int rank, const int ranks) {
+    const size_t base = nz / static_cast<size_t>(ranks);
+    const size_t extra = nz % static_cast<size_t>(ranks);
+    const size_t r = static_cast<size_t>(rank);
+    return {r * base + std::min(r, extra), base + (r < extra ? 1U : 0U)};
+}
+
+static void initializeGrid(std::vector<Real>& grid, const size_t nx,
+                           const size_t ny, const Slab slab) {
+    const size_t plane = nx * ny;
+    for (size_t lz = 1; lz <= slab.nz; ++lz) {
+        const size_t globalBase = (slab.firstZ + lz - 1) * plane;
+        Real* const dst = grid.data() + lz * plane;
+        for (size_t i = 0; i < plane; ++i)
+            dst[i] = static_cast<Real>((globalBase + i) % 19);
+    }
+}
+
+// Update one owned plane.  Physical boundary cells retain their input value.
+static inline void updatePlane(const Real* input, Real* output,
+                               const size_t lz, const size_t globalZ,
+                               const size_t nx, const size_t ny,
+                               const size_t globalNz) {
+    const size_t plane = nx * ny;
+    const size_t base = lz * plane;
+    if (globalZ == 0 || globalZ + 1 == globalNz) {
+        std::memcpy(output + base, input + base, plane * sizeof(Real));
+        return;
+    }
+
+    // Copy the four xy boundary edges (corners may harmlessly be copied twice).
+    std::memcpy(output + base, input + base, nx * sizeof(Real));
+    std::memcpy(output + base + (ny - 1) * nx,
+                input + base + (ny - 1) * nx, nx * sizeof(Real));
+    for (size_t y = 1; y + 1 < ny; ++y) {
+        output[base + y * nx] = input[base + y * nx];
+        output[base + y * nx + nx - 1] = input[base + y * nx + nx - 1];
+    }
+
+    constexpr Real oneSeventh = Real{1.0 / 7.0};
+    for (size_t y = 1; y + 1 < ny; ++y) {
+        const size_t row = base + y * nx;
+        for (size_t x = 1; x + 1 < nx; ++x) {
+            const size_t i = row + x;
+            output[i] = (input[i] + input[i - 1] + input[i + 1] +
+                         input[i - nx] + input[i + nx] +
+                         input[i - plane] + input[i + plane]) * oneSeventh;
         }
     }
 }

+    if (rank + 1 < ranks) {

+                  &requests[nreq++]);
+        MPI_Isend(input.data() + slab.nz * plane, static_cast<int>(plane),
+                  MPI_DOUBLE, rank + 1, 101, comm, &requests[nreq++]);
     }
+
+    // These planes depend only on locally owned data, overlapping halo traffic.

-void initializeGrid(std::vector<Real>& grid, const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz) {
-    for (size_t z = 0; z < nz; ++z) {

+struct Slab {
+    size_t firstZ;
+    size_t nz;
+};
+
+static Slab decompose(const size_t nz, const int rank, const int ranks) {
+    const size_t base = nz / static_cast<size_t>(ranks);
+    const size_t extra = nz % static_cast<size_t>(ranks);
+    const size_t r = static_cast<size_t>(rank);
+    return {r * base + std::min(r, extra), base + (r < extra ? 1U : 0U)};
+}
+
+static void initializeGrid(std::vector<Real>& grid, const size_t nx,
+                           const size_t ny, const Slab slab) {
+    const size_t plane = nx * ny;
+    for (size_t lz = 1; lz <= slab.nz; ++lz) {
+        const size_t globalBase = (slab.firstZ + lz - 1) * plane;
+        Real* const dst = grid.data() + lz * plane;
+        for (size_t i = 0; i < plane; ++i)
+            dst[i] = static_cast<Real>((globalBase + i) % 19);
+    }
+}
+
+// Update one owned plane.  Physical boundary cells retain their input value.
+static inline void updatePlane(const Real* input, Real* output,
+                               const size_t lz, const size_t globalZ,
+                               const size_t nx, const size_t ny,
+                               const size_t globalNz) {
+    const size_t plane = nx * ny;
+    const size_t base = lz * plane;
+    if (globalZ == 0 || globalZ + 1 == globalNz) {
+        std::memcpy(output + base, input + base, plane * sizeof(Real));
+        return;
+    }
+
+    // Copy the four xy boundary edges (corners may harmlessly be copied twice).
+    std::memcpy(output + base, input + base, nx * sizeof(Real));
+    std::memcpy(output + base + (ny - 1) * nx,
+                input + base + (ny - 1) * nx, nx * sizeof(Real));
+    for (size_t y = 1; y + 1 < ny; ++y) {
+        output[base + y * nx] = input[base + y * nx];
+        output[base + y * nx + nx - 1] = input[base + y * nx + nx - 1];
+    }
+
+    constexpr Real oneSeventh = Real{1.0 / 7.0};
+    for (size_t y = 1; y + 1 < ny; ++y) {
+        const size_t row = base + y * nx;
+        for (size_t x = 1; x + 1 < nx; ++x) {
+            const size_t i = row + x;
+            output[i] = (input[i] + input[i - 1] + input[i + 1] +
+                         input[i - nx] + input[i + nx] +
+                         input[i - plane] + input[i + plane]) * oneSeventh;
         }
     }
 }

+static void stencilIteration(std::vector<Real>& input,
+                             std::vector<Real>& output, const size_t nx,
+                             const size_t ny, const size_t globalNz,
+                             const Slab slab, const int rank, const int ranks,
+                             MPI_Comm comm) {
+    const size_t plane = nx * ny;
+    MPI_Request requests[4];
+    int nreq = 0;
+
+    // Receive directly into ghost planes and send directly from owned planes.
+    if (rank > 0) {
+        MPI_Irecv(input.data(), static_cast<int>(plane),
+                  MPI_DOUBLE, rank - 1, 101, comm, &requests[nreq++]);
+        MPI_Isend(input.data() + plane, static_cast<int>(plane), MPI_DOUBLE,
+                  rank - 1, 102, comm, &requests[nreq++]);
     }
-    
-    // Copy boundary values
-    for (size_t z = 0; z < nz; ++z) {
-        for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {
-            for (size_t x = 0; x < nx; ++x) {
-                if (x == 0 || x == nx-1 || y == 0 || y == ny-1 || z == 0 || z == nz-1) {
-                    const size_t idx = idx3(x, y, z, nx, ny);
-                    output[idx] = input[idx];
-                }
-            }
-        }
+    if (rank + 1 < ranks) {
+        MPI_Irecv(input.data() + (slab.nz + 1) * plane,
+                  static_cast<int>(plane), MPI_DOUBLE, rank + 1, 102, comm,
+                  &requests[nreq++]);
+        MPI_Isend(input.data() + slab.nz * plane, static_cast<int>(plane),
+                  MPI_DOUBLE, rank + 1, 101, comm, &requests[nreq++]);
     }
+
+    // These planes depend only on locally owned data, overlapping halo traffic.
+    for (size_t lz = 2; lz < slab.nz; ++lz)
+        updatePlane(input.data(), output.data(), lz, slab.firstZ + lz - 1,
+                    nx, ny, globalNz);
+
+    if (nreq != 0)
+        MPI_Waitall(nreq, requests, MPI_STATUSES_IGNORE);
+
+    updatePlane(input.data(), output.data(), 1, slab.firstZ,
+                nx, ny, globalNz);
+    if (slab.nz > 1)
+        updatePlane(input.data(), output.data(), slab.nz,
+                    slab.firstZ + slab.nz - 1, nx, ny, globalNz);
 }

-void initializeGrid(std::vector<Real>& grid, const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz) {
-    for (size_t z = 0; z < nz; ++z) {
-        for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {

-                const size_t idx = idx3(x, y, z, nx, ny);
-                grid[idx] = (idx % 19) * 1.0;
-            }
+struct Slab {
+    size_t firstZ;
+    size_t nz;
+};
+
+static Slab decompose(const size_t nz, const int rank, const int ranks) {
+    const size_t base = nz / static_cast<size_t>(ranks);
+    const size_t extra = nz % static_cast<size_t>(ranks);
+    const size_t r = static_cast<size_t>(rank);
+    return {r * base + std::min(r, extra), base + (r < extra ? 1U : 0U)};
+}
+
+static void initializeGrid(std::vector<Real>& grid, const size_t nx,
+                           const size_t ny, const Slab slab) {
+    const size_t plane = nx * ny;
+    for (size_t lz = 1; lz <= slab.nz; ++lz) {
+        const size_t globalBase = (slab.firstZ + lz - 1) * plane;
+        Real* const dst = grid.data() + lz * plane;
+        for (size_t i = 0; i < plane; ++i)
+            dst[i] = static_cast<Real>((globalBase + i) % 19);
+    }
+}
+
+// Update one owned plane.  Physical boundary cells retain their input value.
+static inline void updatePlane(const Real* input, Real* output,
+                               const size_t lz, const size_t globalZ,
+                               const size_t nx, const size_t ny,
+                               const size_t globalNz) {
+    const size_t plane = nx * ny;
+    const size_t base = lz * plane;
+    if (globalZ == 0 || globalZ + 1 == globalNz) {
+        std::memcpy(output + base, input + base, plane * sizeof(Real));
+        return;
+    }
+
+    // Copy the four xy boundary edges (corners may harmlessly be copied twice).
+    std::memcpy(output + base, input + base, nx * sizeof(Real));
+    std::memcpy(output + base + (ny - 1) * nx,
+                input + base + (ny - 1) * nx, nx * sizeof(Real));
+    for (size_t y = 1; y + 1 < ny; ++y) {
+        output[base + y * nx] = input[base + y * nx];
+        output[base + y * nx + nx - 1] = input[base + y * nx + nx - 1];
+    }
+
+    constexpr Real oneSeventh = Real{1.0 / 7.0};
+    for (size_t y = 1; y + 1 < ny; ++y) {
+        const size_t row = base + y * nx;
+        for (size_t x = 1; x + 1 < nx; ++x) {
+            const size_t i = row + x;
+            output[i] = (input[i] + input[i - 1] + input[i + 1] +
+                         input[i - nx] + input[i + nx] +
+                         input[i - plane] + input[i + plane]) * oneSeventh;
         }
     }
 }

+static void stencilIteration(std::vector<Real>& input,
+                             std::vector<Real>& output, const size_t nx,
+                             const size_t ny, const size_t globalNz,
+                             const Slab slab, const int rank, const int ranks,
+                             MPI_Comm comm) {
+    const size_t plane = nx * ny;
+    MPI_Request requests[4];
+    int nreq = 0;
+
+    // Receive directly into ghost planes and send directly from owned planes.
+    if (rank > 0) {
+        MPI_Irecv(input.data(), static_cast<int>(plane),
+                  MPI_DOUBLE, rank - 1, 101, comm, &requests[nreq++]);
+        MPI_Isend(input.data() + plane, static_cast<int>(plane), MPI_DOUBLE,
+                  rank - 1, 102, comm, &requests[nreq++]);
     }
-    
-    // Copy boundary values
-    for (size_t z = 0; z < nz; ++z) {
-        for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {
-            for (size_t x = 0; x < nx; ++x) {
-                if (x == 0 || x == nx-1 || y == 0 || y == ny-1 || z == 0 || z == nz-1) {
-                    const size_t idx = idx3(x, y, z, nx, ny);
-                    output[idx] = input[idx];
-                }
-            }
-        }
+    if (rank + 1 < ranks) {
+        MPI_Irecv(input.data() + (slab.nz + 1) * plane,
+                  static_cast<int>(plane), MPI_DOUBLE, rank + 1, 102, comm,
+                  &requests[nreq++]);
+        MPI_Isend(input.data() + slab.nz * plane, static_cast<int>(plane),
+                  MPI_DOUBLE, rank + 1, 101, comm, &requests[nreq++]);
     }
+
+    // These planes depend only on locally owned data, overlapping halo traffic.
+    for (size_t lz = 2; lz < slab.nz; ++lz)
+        updatePlane(input.data(), output.data(), lz, slab.firstZ + lz - 1,
+                    nx, ny, globalNz);
+
+    if (nreq != 0)
+        MPI_Waitall(nreq, requests, MPI_STATUSES_IGNORE);
+
+    updatePlane(input.data(), output.data(), 1, slab.firstZ,
+                nx, ny, globalNz);
+    if (slab.nz > 1)
+        updatePlane(input.data(), output.data(), slab.nz,
+                    slab.firstZ + slab.nz - 1, nx, ny, globalNz);
 }

+static void initializeGrid(std::vector<Real>& grid, const size_t nx,
+                           const size_t ny, const Slab slab) {
+    const size_t plane = nx * ny;
+    for (size_t lz = 1; lz <= slab.nz; ++lz) {
+        const size_t globalBase = (slab.firstZ + lz - 1) * plane;
+        Real* const dst = grid.data() + lz * plane;
+        for (size_t i = 0; i < plane; ++i)
+            dst[i] = static_cast<Real>((globalBase + i) % 19);
+    }
+}
+
+// Update one owned plane.  Physical boundary cells retain their input value.
+static inline void updatePlane(const Real* input, Real* output,
+                               const size_t lz, const size_t globalZ,
+                               const size_t nx, const size_t ny,
+                               const size_t globalNz) {
+    const size_t plane = nx * ny;
+    const size_t base = lz * plane;
+    if (globalZ == 0 || globalZ + 1 == globalNz) {
+        std::memcpy(output + base, input + base, plane * sizeof(Real));
+        return;
+    }
+
+    // Copy the four xy boundary edges (corners may harmlessly be copied twice).
+    std::memcpy(output + base, input + base, nx * sizeof(Real));
+    std::memcpy(output + base + (ny - 1) * nx,
+                input + base + (ny - 1) * nx, nx * sizeof(Real));
+    for (size_t y = 1; y + 1 < ny; ++y) {
+        output[base + y * nx] = input[base + y * nx];
+        output[base + y * nx + nx - 1] = input[base + y * nx + nx - 1];
+    }
+
+    constexpr Real oneSeventh = Real{1.0 / 7.0};
+    for (size_t y = 1; y + 1 < ny; ++y) {
+        const size_t row = base + y * nx;
+        for (size_t x = 1; x + 1 < nx; ++x) {
+            const size_t i = row + x;
+            output[i] = (input[i] + input[i - 1] + input[i + 1] +
+                         input[i - nx] + input[i + nx] +
+                         input[i - plane] + input[i + plane]) * oneSeventh;
         }
     }
 }

-                const size_t idx = idx3(x, y, z, nx, ny);
-

+static void stencilIteration(std::vector<Real>& input,
+                             std::vector<Real>& output, const size_t nx,
+                             const size_t ny, const size_t globalNz,
+                             const Slab slab, const int rank, const int ranks,
+                             MPI_Comm comm) {
+    const size_t plane = nx * ny;
+    MPI_Request requests[4];
+    int nreq = 0;
+
+    // Receive directly into ghost planes and send directly from owned planes.
+    if (rank > 0) {
+        MPI_Irecv(input.data(), static_cast<int>(plane),
+                  MPI_DOUBLE, rank - 1, 101, comm, &requests[nreq++]);
+        MPI_Isend(input.data() + plane, static_cast<int>(plane), MPI_DOUBLE,
+                  rank - 1, 102, comm, &requests[nreq++]);
     }
-    
-    // Copy boundary values
-    for (size_t z = 0; z < nz; ++z) {
-        for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {
-            for (size_t x = 0; x < nx; ++x) {
-                if (x == 0 || x == nx-1 || y == 0 || y == ny-1 || z == 0 || z == nz-1) {
-                    const size_t idx = idx3(x, y, z, nx, ny);
-                    output[idx] = input[idx];
-                }
-            }
-        }
+    if (rank + 1 < ranks) {
+        MPI_Irecv(input.data() + (slab.nz + 1) * plane,
+                  static_cast<int>(plane), MPI_DOUBLE, rank + 1, 102, comm,
+                  &requests[nreq++]);
+        MPI_Isend(input.data() + slab.nz * plane, static_cast<int>(plane),
+                  MPI_DOUBLE, rank + 1, 101, comm, &requests[nreq++]);
     }
+
+    // These planes depend only on locally owned data, overlapping halo traffic.
+    for (size_t lz = 2; lz < slab.nz; ++lz)
+        updatePlane(input.data(), output.data(), lz, slab.firstZ + lz - 1,
+                    nx, ny, globalNz);
+
+    if (nreq != 0)
+        MPI_Waitall(nreq, requests, MPI_STATUSES_IGNORE);
+
+    updatePlane(input.data(), output.data(), 1, slab.firstZ,
+                nx, ny, globalNz);
+    if (slab.nz > 1)
+        updatePlane(input.data(), output.data(), slab.nz,
+                    slab.firstZ + slab.nz - 1, nx, ny, globalNz);
 }

+struct Slab {
+    size_t firstZ;
+    size_t nz;
+};
+
+static Slab decompose(const size_t nz, const int rank, const int ranks) {
+    const size_t base = nz / static_cast<size_t>(ranks);
+    const size_t extra = nz % static_cast<size_t>(ranks);
+    const size_t r = static_cast<size_t>(rank);
+    return {r * base + std::min(r, extra), base + (r < extra ? 1U : 0U)};
+}
+
+static void initializeGrid(std::vector<Real>& grid, const size_t nx,
+                           const size_t ny, const Slab slab) {
+    const size_t plane = nx * ny;
+    for (size_t lz = 1; lz <= slab.nz; ++lz) {
+        const size_t globalBase = (slab.firstZ + lz - 1) * plane;
+        Real* const dst = grid.data() + lz * plane;
+        for (size_t i = 0; i < plane; ++i)
+            dst[i] = static_cast<Real>((globalBase + i) % 19);
+    }
+}
+
+// Update one owned plane.  Physical boundary cells retain their input value.
+static inline void updatePlane(const Real* input, Real* output,
+                               const size_t lz, const size_t globalZ,
+                               const size_t nx, const size_t ny,
+                               const size_t globalNz) {
+    const size_t plane = nx * ny;
+    const size_t base = lz * plane;
+    if (globalZ == 0 || globalZ + 1 == globalNz) {
+        std::memcpy(output + base, input + base, plane * sizeof(Real));
+        return;
+    }
+
+    // Copy the four xy boundary edges (corners may harmlessly be copied twice).
+    std::memcpy(output + base, input + base, nx * sizeof(Real));
+    std::memcpy(output + base + (ny - 1) * nx,
+                input + base + (ny - 1) * nx, nx * sizeof(Real));
+    for (size_t y = 1; y + 1 < ny; ++y) {
+        output[base + y * nx] = input[base + y * nx];
+        output[base + y * nx + nx - 1] = input[base + y * nx + nx - 1];
+    }
+
+    constexpr Real oneSeventh = Real{1.0 / 7.0};
+    for (size_t y = 1; y + 1 < ny; ++y) {
+        const size_t row = base + y * nx;
+        for (size_t x = 1; x + 1 < nx; ++x) {
+            const size_t i = row + x;
+            output[i] = (input[i] + input[i - 1] + input[i + 1] +
+                         input[i - nx] + input[i + nx] +
+                         input[i - plane] + input[i + plane]) * oneSeventh;
         }
     }
 }

+                  MPI_DOUBLE, rank + 1, 101, comm, &requests[nreq++]);

+    for (size_t lz = 2; lz < slab.nz; ++lz)

+struct Slab {
+    size_t firstZ;
+    size_t nz;
+};
+
+static Slab decompose(const size_t nz, const int rank, const int ranks) {
+    const size_t base = nz / static_cast<size_t>(ranks);
+    const size_t extra = nz % static_cast<size_t>(ranks);
+    const size_t r = static_cast<size_t>(rank);
+    return {r * base + std::min(r, extra), base + (r < extra ? 1U : 0U)};
+}
+
+static void initializeGrid(std::vector<Real>& grid, const size_t nx,
+                           const size_t ny, const Slab slab) {
+    const size_t plane = nx * ny;
+    for (size_t lz = 1; lz <= slab.nz; ++lz) {
+        const size_t globalBase = (slab.firstZ + lz - 1) * plane;
+        Real* const dst = grid.data() + lz * plane;
+        for (size_t i = 0; i < plane; ++i)
+            dst[i] = static_cast<Real>((globalBase + i) % 19);
+    }
+}
+
+// Update one owned plane.  Physical boundary cells retain their input value.
+static inline void updatePlane(const Real* input, Real* output,
+                               const size_t lz, const size_t globalZ,
+                               const size_t nx, const size_t ny,
+                               const size_t globalNz) {
+    const size_t plane = nx * ny;
+    const size_t base = lz * plane;
+    if (globalZ == 0 || globalZ + 1 == globalNz) {
+        std::memcpy(output + base, input + base, plane * sizeof(Real));
+        return;
+    }
+
+    // Copy the four xy boundary edges (corners may harmlessly be copied twice).
+    std::memcpy(output + base, input + base, nx * sizeof(Real));
+    std::memcpy(output + base + (ny - 1) * nx,
+                input + base + (ny - 1) * nx, nx * sizeof(Real));
+    for (size_t y = 1; y + 1 < ny; ++y) {
+        output[base + y * nx] = input[base + y * nx];
+        output[base + y * nx + nx - 1] = input[base + y * nx + nx - 1];
+    }
+
+    constexpr Real oneSeventh = Real{1.0 / 7.0};
+    for (size_t y = 1; y + 1 < ny; ++y) {
+        const size_t row = base + y * nx;
+        for (size_t x = 1; x + 1 < nx; ++x) {
+            const size_t i = row + x;
+            output[i] = (input[i] + input[i - 1] + input[i + 1] +
+                         input[i - nx] + input[i + nx] +
+                         input[i - plane] + input[i + plane]) * oneSeventh;
         }
     }
 }

+static void stencilIteration(std::vector<Real>& input,
+                             std::vector<Real>& output, const size_t nx,
+                             const size_t ny, const size_t globalNz,
+                             const Slab slab, const int rank, const int ranks,
+                             MPI_Comm comm) {
+    const size_t plane = nx * ny;
+    MPI_Request requests[4];
+    int nreq = 0;
+
+    // Receive directly into ghost planes and send directly from owned planes.
+    if (rank > 0) {
+        MPI_Irecv(input.data(), static_cast<int>(plane),
+                  MPI_DOUBLE, rank - 1, 101, comm, &requests[nreq++]);
+        MPI_Isend(input.data() + plane, static_cast<int>(plane), MPI_DOUBLE,
+                  rank - 1, 102, comm, &requests[nreq++]);
     }
-    
-    // Copy boundary values
-    for (size_t z = 0; z < nz; ++z) {
-        for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {
-            for (size_t x = 0; x < nx; ++x) {
-                if (x == 0 || x == nx-1 || y == 0 || y == ny-1 || z == 0 || z == nz-1) {
-                    const size_t idx = idx3(x, y, z, nx, ny);
-                    output[idx] = input[idx];
-                }
-            }
-        }
+    if (rank + 1 < ranks) {
+        MPI_Irecv(input.data() + (slab.nz + 1) * plane,
+                  static_cast<int>(plane), MPI_DOUBLE, rank + 1, 102, comm,
+                  &requests[nreq++]);
+        MPI_Isend(input.data() + slab.nz * plane, static_cast<int>(plane),
+                  MPI_DOUBLE, rank + 1, 101, comm, &requests[nreq++]);
     }
+
+    // These planes depend only on locally owned data, overlapping halo traffic.
+    for (size_t lz = 2; lz < slab.nz; ++lz)
+        updatePlane(input.data(), output.data(), lz, slab.firstZ + lz - 1,
+                    nx, ny, globalNz);
+
+    if (nreq != 0)
+        MPI_Waitall(nreq, requests, MPI_STATUSES_IGNORE);
+
+    updatePlane(input.data(), output.data(), 1, slab.firstZ,
+                nx, ny, globalNz);
+    if (slab.nz > 1)
+        updatePlane(input.data(), output.data(), slab.nz,
+                    slab.firstZ + slab.nz - 1, nx, ny, globalNz);
 }

codex
Implemented MPI distributed-memory parallelism in:

- [stencil3d.cpp](/home/llmtest/evals/20260805-120633/stencil3d_gpt-5.6-sol-medium_mpi_r3/stencil3d/stencil3d.cpp)
- [CMakeLists.txt](/home/llmtest/evals/20260805-120633/stencil3d_gpt-5.6-sol-medium_mpi_r3/stencil3d/CMakeLists.txt)

Key features:

- Balanced z-slab domain decomposition
- Nonblocking halo exchange with computation overlap
- Distributed initialization and validation
- Maximum-rank timing via `MPI_Wtime`
- Results gathered only when `-r` is requested
- Handles uneven decompositions and more ranks than z planes
- Preserves executable name and output semantics

Verification completed:

- Clean warning-free CMake build
- Identical hashes/results for 1 and 4 MPI ranks
- Tested uneven dimensions
- Tested 6 processes with only 3 z planes
- Default 128³, 10-iteration validation passed bit-for-bit across rank counts

+struct Slab {

+    size_t nz;

+static Slab decompose(const size_t nz, const int rank, const int ranks) {
+    const size_t base = nz / static_cast<size_t>(ranks);
+    const size_t extra = nz % static_cast<size_t>(ranks);
+    const size_t r = static_cast<size_t>(rank);
+    return {r * base + std::min(r, extra), base + (r < extra ? 1U : 0U)};
+}
+
+static void initializeGrid(std::vector<Real>& grid, const size_t nx,
+                           const size_t ny, const Slab slab) {
+    const size_t plane = nx * ny;
+    for (size_t lz = 1; lz <= slab.nz; ++lz) {
+        const size_t globalBase = (slab.firstZ + lz - 1) * plane;
+        Real* const dst = grid.data() + lz * plane;
+        for (size_t i = 0; i < plane; ++i)
+            dst[i] = static_cast<Real>((globalBase + i) % 19);
+    }
+}
+
+// Update one owned plane.  Physical boundary cells retain their input value.
+static inline void updatePlane(const Real* input, Real* output,
+                               const size_t lz, const size_t globalZ,
+                               const size_t nx, const size_t ny,
+                               const size_t globalNz) {
+    const size_t plane = nx * ny;
+    const size_t base = lz * plane;
+    if (globalZ == 0 || globalZ + 1 == globalNz) {
+        std::memcpy(output + base, input + base, plane * sizeof(Real));
+        return;
+    }
+
+    // Copy the four xy boundary edges (corners may harmlessly be copied twice).
+    std::memcpy(output + base, input + base, nx * sizeof(Real));
+    std::memcpy(output + base + (ny - 1) * nx,
+                input + base + (ny - 1) * nx, nx * sizeof(Real));
+    for (size_t y = 1; y + 1 < ny; ++y) {
+        output[base + y * nx] = input[base + y * nx];
+        output[base + y * nx + nx - 1] = input[base + y * nx + nx - 1];
+    }
+
+    constexpr Real oneSeventh = Real{1.0 / 7.0};
+    for (size_t y = 1; y + 1 < ny; ++y) {
+        const size_t row = base + y * nx;
+        for (size_t x = 1; x + 1 < nx; ++x) {
+            const size_t i = row + x;
+            output[i] = (input[i] + input[i - 1] + input[i + 1] +
+                         input[i - nx] + input[i + nx] +
+                         input[i - plane] + input[i + plane]) * oneSeventh;
         }
     }
 }

+static void stencilIteration(std::vector<Real>& input,
+                             std::vector<Real>& output, const size_t nx,
+                             const size_t ny, const size_t globalNz,
+                             const Slab slab, const int rank, const int ranks,
+                             MPI_Comm comm) {
+    const size_t plane = nx * ny;
+    MPI_Request requests[4];
+    int nreq = 0;
+
+    // Receive directly into ghost planes and send directly from owned planes.

+                  MPI_DOUBLE, rank - 1, 101, comm, &requests[nreq++]);

+                  static_cast<int>(plane), MPI_DOUBLE, rank + 1, 102, comm,
+                  &requests[nreq++]);

+                  MPI_DOUBLE, rank + 1, 101, comm, &requests[nreq++]);

+                    slab.firstZ + slab.nz - 1, nx, ny, globalNz);

Implemented MPI distributed-memory parallelism in:

- [stencil3d.cpp](/home/llmtest/evals/20260805-120633/stencil3d_gpt-5.6-sol-medium_mpi_r3/stencil3d/stencil3d.cpp)
- [CMakeLists.txt](/home/llmtest/evals/20260805-120633/stencil3d_gpt-5.6-sol-medium_mpi_r3/stencil3d/CMakeLists.txt)

Key features:

- Balanced z-slab domain decomposition
- Nonblocking halo exchange with computation overlap
- Distributed initialization and validation
- Maximum-rank timing via `MPI_Wtime`
- Results gathered only when `-r` is requested
- Handles uneven decompositions and more ranks than z planes
- Preserves executable name and output semantics

Verification completed:

- Clean warning-free CMake build
- Identical hashes/results for 1 and 4 MPI ranks
- Tested uneven dimensions
- Tested 6 processes with only 3 z planes
- Default 128³, 10-iteration validation passed bit-for-bit across rank counts
